APPENDIX L KOWLOON AND LAPPA: QUARTERLY RETURNS OF TRADE. JANUARY-MARCH 1897.
176 CUTM GA T1.[JANi-MAR.9 (BtVfsttd 23td April 1897.) .9 CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, i7m APRIL 1897. SIR, I.-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 38z, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbliuring stations in Native vessels for the first quarter of the year 1897, January-March. b. The Custom Iouse (:L 31 a) was opened on the 2nd April 1887, and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, began on that day. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.2b 3o and Hk.ft go por picul) commenced on the 14th April 1887, and the collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates ixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the IHoppo, on the Iat July 1887. The stations at present controlled by the Kowloon Customs are known as the Capsuimoon (t 9), Changehow (F ), Fotochow (i P #), and Kowloon (IL f1) stations. c. The word &quot; Inwards &quot; in these statistics mdans t6 China, and the wM1 &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II. HILLIER, Commissioner of Custom. To the INSPECTOR GENERAL OF CUSTOMS.
1897.] LAPPA. 187 (Received 2 1 st April 1897.) CUSTOM HOUSE, LAPPA, 14TH APRIL 1897. SIR, I.--a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the first quarter of the year 1897, January-March. b.. The Custom House ( ) was opened on the 2nd April 1887. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.nh 30 and Hk.TM 8o per picul) and the collection of Provincial Likinand Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, com- menced on that day. The collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, commenced on the Ist July 1887. The stations at present controlled by the Lappa Customs are known, as the Malowchow (A I N) and Chienshan (% ii) stations; with three sub-stations, known as the Shekkok (5 ), Kuttai (- *), and Kwanchiap (W Fp) sub-stations. c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China. II.
